[Graveyard Gate]
Along the main path two small buildings are visible to the west. To the east a burnt building with a towering steeple looks down across the graveyard.  An old shack rests comfortably framing a single broken window.  The old and cracked front door is flanked by a shovel and pick axe worn and splintered from use. 

The area is illuminated.

[Graveyard]
Nothing but gravestones covers the north as far as the eye can see, random in size and shape.  To the east peeking above the horizon a dark steeple stands solemn. A rotting fence which branches east and west stretches ominously into the distance.  A thunderous silence contaminates the air.

The area is illuminated.

[Anti-room]
Red veined black marble walls etched with odd symbols, meet with white marble floors which have been cracked and scratched over time.  Thirteen spent red candles, wax covered in dust; display themselves from a large black iron chandelier,which hangs from thick braided black rope, wrapped around a mahogany cleat on the northern wall. Dust several inches deep covers the majority of the floor however; deep footprints in the dust indicate recent activity. An old wooden broom covered with thick cobwebs leans against the corner of the southern wall.

The area is illuminated.

[Mausoleum]
The marble walls frame polished granite tomb markings on all four walls; some which have fallen free and lie broken on the floor.   The remaining tombs display symbols drawn in blood.  In the middle of the northern wall one tomb stands out from the rest in the shape of an oblong circle that encapsulates a smaller circle, and looks like an eye.  Beneath the symbol a single word is written with blood in common.  Upon closer inspection the word spells "eternal".

The area is illuminated.

[Mausoleum]
Several benches crafted from stone rest against each wall. Candle holders,three mounted to each wall horizontally,display spent candles;the center holder,a red one accompanied by a black one on each side. A large circle drawn in the dusty floor displays an eye like symbol in its center.  Black and red candles recently burnt, alternate around the circles circumference; three red candles placed vertically make up the eyes center. Each of the cracked walls appears to have been broken simultaneously as evidenced by the nearly identical spider web pattern each wall displays. In the center of the western wall a single word written in what appears to be blood says "eminent"

[Mausoleum]
Small tomb markers set six across and six down line each of the four walls.  Cracked but cleared of dust and cobwebs the markers contain no words or symbols almost as if each resting place is empty.  Red symbols mark each of the 144 markings.  In the center of the western wall on one of the markers a single word is written in red and says "is"

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: south

[Mausoleum]
Several benches crafted from stone rest against each wall. Candle holders,three mounted to each wall horizontally,display spent candles;the center holder,a red one accompanied by a black one on each side. A large circle drawn in the dusty floor displays an eye like symbol in its center.  Black and red candles recently burnt, alternate around the circles circumference; three red candles placed vertically make up the eyes center. Each of the cracked walls appears to have been broken simultaneously as evidenced by the nearly identical spider web pattern each wall displays. In the center of the western wall a single word written in what appears to be blood says "eminent"

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Sammoth the Rock Troll
Obvious exits are: north, east, south

[Mausoleum]
The marble walls frame polished granite tomb markings on all four walls; some which have fallen free and lie broken on the floor.   The remaining tombs display symbols drawn in blood.  In the middle of the northern wall one tomb stands out from the rest in the shape of an oblong circle that encapsulates a smaller circle, and looks like an eye.  Beneath the symbol a single word is written with blood in common.  Upon closer inspection the word spells "eternal".

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Sammoth the Rock Troll
Obvious exits are: south, west

[Anti-room]
Red veined black marble walls etched with odd symbols, meet with white marble floors which have been cracked and scratched over time.  Thirteen spent red candles, wax covered in dust; display themselves from a large black iron chandelier,which hangs from thick braided black rope, wrapped around a mahogany cleat on the northern wall. Dust several inches deep covers the majority of the floor however; deep footprints in the dust indicate recent activity. An old wooden broom covered with thick cobwebs leans against the corner of the southern wall.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Sammoth the Rock Troll, 1st lunatic cultist, 1st frightened ghost
Obvious exits are: north, south, arch

Red veined black marble walls etched with odd symbols, meet with white marble floors which have been cracked and scratched over time.  Thirteen spent red candles, wax covered in dust; display themselves from a large black iron chandelier,which hangs from thick braided black rope, wrapped around a mahogany cleat on the northern wall. Dust several inches deep covers the majority of the floor however; deep footprints in the dust indicate recent activity. An old wooden broom covered with thick cobwebs leans against the corner of the southern wall.

[Mausoleum]
The marble walls frame polished granite tomb markings on all four walls; some which have fallen free and lie broken on the floor.   The remaining tombs display symbols drawn in blood.  In the middle of the northern wall one tomb stands out from the rest in the shape of an oblong circle that encapsulates a smaller circle, and looks like an eye.  Beneath the symbol a single word is written with blood in common.  Upon closer inspection the word spells "eternal".

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Sammoth the Rock Troll, 1st lunatic cultist
Obvious exits are: south, west

[Mausoleum]
Large black marble pedestals which display ornately carved caskets, center themselves in this unusually large room.   Black, red and white jewels configured in a meticulous circular pattern line the black marble walls on all four sides.  Strange symbols similar to those in the other rooms appear on each casket.  On the center casket a single word again written in common from what appears to be blood says "life"

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Sammoth the Rock Troll, 1st lunatic cultist, 2nd lunatic cultist, 1st frightened ghost

[Mausoleum]
The marble walls frame polished granite tomb markings on all four walls; some which have fallen free and lie broken on the floor.   The remaining tombs display symbols drawn in blood.  In the middle of the northern wall one tomb stands out from the rest in the shape of an oblong circle that encapsulates a smaller circle, and looks like an eye.  Beneath the symbol a single word is written with blood in common.  Upon closer inspection the word spells "eternal".

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Sammoth the Rock Troll
Obvious exits are: south, west

[Mausoleum]
Large black marble pedestals which display ornately carved caskets, center themselves in this unusually large room.   Black, red and white jewels configured in a meticulous circular pattern line the black marble walls on all four sides.  Strange symbols similar to those in the other rooms appear on each casket.  On the center casket a single word again written in common from what appears to be blood says "life"

[Anti-room]
Red veined black marble walls etched with odd symbols, meet with white marble floors which have been cracked and scratched over time.  Thirteen spent red candles, wax covered in dust; display themselves from a large black iron chandelier,which hangs from thick braided black rope, wrapped around a mahogany cleat on the northern wall. Dust several inches deep covers the majority of the floor however; deep footprints in the dust indicate recent activity. An old wooden broom covered with thick cobwebs leans against the corner of the southern wall.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Sammoth the Rock Troll
Obvious exits are: north, south, arch

Sammoth's group just went north.

You follow Sammoth...


[Mausoleum]
The marble walls frame polished granite tomb markings on all four walls; some which have fallen free and lie broken on the floor.   The remaining tombs display symbols drawn in blood.  In the middle of the northern wall one tomb stands out from the rest in the shape of an oblong circle that encapsulates a smaller circle, and looks like an eye.  Beneath the symbol a single word is written with blood in common.  Upon closer inspection the word spells "eternal".

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Sammoth the Rock Troll, 1st lunatic cultist
Obvious exits are: south, west

[Mausoleum]
Several benches crafted from stone rest against each wall. Candle holders,three mounted to each wall horizontally,display spent candles;the center holder,a red one accompanied by a black one on each side. A large circle drawn in the dusty floor displays an eye like symbol in its center.  Black and red candles recently burnt, alternate around the circles circumference; three red candles placed vertically make up the eyes center. Each of the cracked walls appears to have been broken simultaneously as evidenced by the nearly identical spider web pattern each wall displays. In the center of the western wall a single word written in what appears to be blood says "eminent"

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Sammoth the Rock Troll
Obvious exits are: north, east, south

Sammoth's group just went south.

You follow Sammoth...


[Passage]
Walls of black marble meet floors of red ceramic tile and heads south. Narrow walls and low ceilings indicate that this passage is not meant to be populated by those who have died. Small wooden torches covered by cob webs line both the eastern and western walls. A mild odor resonates from the south.  Blood trails mimicking those of a dragged body trail to the south.

The area is illuminated.

[Chamber]
Blood splattered walls merge with blood stained floors. The symbol of an eye repeats over and over on each walls as if etched by hand with a small sharp dagger.  Pools of blood lie scattered about the floor, and scorched bodies hang upside down bound at the feet from large lengths of red braided rope.  A strong odor of burnt flesh hovers ominously across the room.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Sammoth the Rock Troll
Obvious exits are: north, south

Sammoth's group just went south.

You follow Sammoth...


[Mass Tomb]
Bodies are strewn about the floor, some in piles some in random. Disemboweled Cyrwian babies hang from the ceiling flanked by what can only be described as their parents, chests pried open their, hearts forced into their mouths. Bloodied and burnt bodies lie in six neat piles in the center of separately blood drawn circles in the room’s center. A wailing can be heard from a small crack in the southern walls which funnels wind through it.

The area is illuminated.

[Mausoleum]
Several benches crafted from stone rest against each wall. Candle holders,three mounted to each wall horizontally,display spent candles;the center holder,a red one accompanied by a black one on each side. [Mausoleum]
Several benches crafted from stone rest against each wall. Candle holders,three mounted to each wall horizontally,display spent candles;the center holder,a red one accompanied by a black one on each side. A large circle drawn in the dusty floor displays an eye like symbol in its center.  Black and red candles recently burnt, alternate around the circles circumference; three red candles placed vertically make up the eyes center. Each of the cracked walls appears to have been broken simultaneously as evidenced by the nearly identical spider web pattern each wall displays. In the center of the western wall a single word written in what appears to be blood says "eminent"

The area is illuminated.

[Mausoleum]
Several benches crafted from stone rest against each wall. Candle holders,three mounted to each wall horizontally,display spent candles;the center holder,a red one accompanied by a black one on each side. A large circle drawn in the dusty floor displays an eye like symbol in its center.  Black and red candles recently burnt, alternate around the circles circumference; three red candles placed vertically make up the eyes center. Each of the cracked walls appears to have been broken simultaneously as evidenced by the nearly identical spider web pattern each wall displays. In the center of the western wall a single word written in what appears to be blood says "eminent"

The area is illuminated.

[Mausoleum]
The marble walls frame polished granite tomb markings on all four walls; some which have fallen free and lie broken on the floor.   The remaining tombs display symbols drawn in blood.  In the middle of the northern wall one tomb stands out from the rest in the shape of an oblong circle that encapsulates a smaller circle, and looks like an eye.  Beneath the symbol a single word is written with blood in common.  Upon closer inspection the word spells "eternal".

The area is illuminated.

[Mausoleum]
The marble walls frame polished granite tomb markings on all four walls; some which have fallen free and lie broken on the floor.   The remaining tombs display symbols drawn in blood.  In the middle of the northern wall one tomb stands out from the rest in the shape of an oblong circle that encapsulates a smaller circle, and looks like an eye.  Beneath the symbol a single word is written with blood in common.  Upon closer inspection the word spells "eternal".

[Anti-room]
Red veined black marble walls etched with odd symbols, meet with white marble floors which have been cracked and scratched over time.  Thirteen spent red candles, wax covered in dust; display themselves from a large black iron chandelier,which hangs from thick braided black rope, wrapped around a mahogany cleat on the northern wall. Dust several inches deep covers the majority of the floor however; deep footprints in the dust indicate recent activity. An old wooden broom covered with thick cobwebs leans against the corner of the southern wall.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Sammoth the Rock Troll, 1st lunatic cultist
Obvious exits are: north, south, arch

[Mausoleum]
The marble walls frame polished granite tomb markings on all four walls; some which have fallen free and lie broken on the floor.   The remaining tombs display symbols drawn in blood.  In the middle of the northern wall one tomb stands out from the rest in the shape of an oblong circle that encapsulates a smaller circle, and looks like an eye.  Beneath the symbol a single word is written with blood in common.  Upon closer inspection the word spells "eternal".

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Sammoth the Rock Troll
Obvious exits are: south, west

Small brick face buildings line the road. Wagons drawn by large horses carry goods both northeast and southwest to their destinations. Dogs bark at the horses in an attempt to move them along the road faster, as those who drive the wagons slap the horses rears with stripped willow sticks. A stooped over crone wanders in from the northeast is is hurriedly shooed back to her appropriate corner.

[Park Avenue]
The well kept homes that line this street are not overly large, but are adequate to house a family in relative security. Houses are set back well off the street and allow a safe area for children to play, women to hang wash, and men to sit on their porch and watch the day pass by idyllically. A glance to the west will find you gazing over a patch of clean thatch that covers the homes below and just beyond the roofs you can see large Olagarner Trees that grow in the park.

[Park Avenue]
Small, yet sturdy and well kept, homes line this cobblestone road. Thatched roofs are fresh and appear to keep out the elements. Each home has a small patch of grass surrounded by a variety of white picket and wrought iron fences. Many of the windows boast flower boxes, and not a single window is without some sort of unique decoration. Towards the west is a large park and a ramp to the east leads up to more housing.

[Park Avenue]

[Central Bank]
A long counter, approximately human chest height, runs the length of the bank. Each window on either side of the bank is completely encased by gilded bars. The unique twisting pattern of the metal attracts your gaze to them, only for your sight to then quickly be pulled upward to the ceiling where a beautiful inlaid ceramic mosaic resides. The bright mosaic proudly depicts the rise of Geddon in all its glory, each noble figure cloaked in gold flake.
